{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_018bW3wcAFWPzLfngwfDgQwB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01UCX36XHjJK9wmF6LXcMr5n","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/analyze_video.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nVideo analysis script for camera motion (egomotion) estimation and dynamic object detection.\nSamples video at 6 fps, estimates camera motion between frames, and detects moving objects.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport cv2\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef sample_video(video_path, target_fps=6):\n    \"\"\"Sample video frames at target fps.\"\"\"\n    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))\n    original_fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)\n    frame_interval = int(original_fps / target_fps)\n\n    frames = []\n    frame_indices = []\n    frame_count = 0\n    sample_count = 0\n\n    while True:\n        ret, frame = cap.read()\n        if not ret:\n            break\n\n        if frame_count % frame_interval == 0:\n            frames.append(frame)\n            frame_indices.append(frame_count)\n            sample_count += 1\n\n        frame_count += 1\n\n    cap.release()\n    print(f\"Sampled {len(frames)} frames from {frame_count} total frames\")\n    print(f\"Original FPS: {original_fps}, Target FPS: {target_fps}, Frame interval: {frame_interval}\")\n\n    return frames, frame_indices\n\n\ndef estimate_camera_motion(frame1, frame2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate camera motion between two frames using optical flow and homography.\n    Returns motion label based on the dominant transformation.\n    \"\"\"\n    gray1 = cv2.cvtColor(frame1, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n    gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n\n    # Detect features\n    orb = cv2.ORB_create(nfeatures=1000)\n    kp1, des1 = orb.detectAndCompute(gray1, None)\n    kp2, des2 = orb.detectAndCompute(gray2, None)\n\n    if des1 is None or des2 is None or len(kp1) < 10 or len(kp2) < 10:\n        return \"Stay\", None, None\n\n    # Match features\n    bf = cv2.BFMatcher(cv2.NORM_HAMMING, crossCheck=True)\n    matches = bf.match(des1, des2)\n\n    if len(matches) < 10:\n        return \"Stay\", None, None\n\n    # Sort matches by distance\n    matches = sorted(matches, key=lambda x: x.distance)\n    good_matches = matches[:min(100, len(matches))]\n\n    # Get matched points\n    pts1 = np.float32([kp1[m.queryIdx].pt for m in good_matches])\n    pts2 = np.float32([kp2[m.trainIdx].pt for m in good_matches])\n\n    # Estimate homography\n    H, mask = cv2.findHomography(pts1, pts2, cv2.RANSAC, 5.0)\n\n    if H is None:\n        return \"Stay\", None, None\n\n    # Analyze homography to determine motion type\n    motion_label = classify_camera_motion(H, frame1.shape)\n\n    return motion_label, H, mask\n\n\ndef classify_camera_motion(H, frame_shape):\n    \"\"\"\n    Classify camera motion based on homography matrix.\n    Returns one of: Stay, Dolly In, Dolly Out, Pan Left, Pan Right, Tilt Up, Tilt Down, Roll Left, Roll Right\n    \"\"\"\n    if H is None:\n        return \"Stay\"\n\n    h, w = frame_shape[:2]\n\n    # Get corner points\n    corners = np.float32([[0, 0], [w, 0], [w, h], [0, h]]).reshape(-1, 1, 2)\n    transformed_corners = cv2.perspectiveTransform(corners, H)\n\n    # Calculate translation vector from center\n    center_src = np.float32([[w/2, h/2]]).reshape(-1, 1, 2)\n    center_dst = cv2.perspectiveTransform(center_src, H)\n\n    dx = center_dst[0, 0, 0] - center_src[0, 0, 0]\n    dy = center_dst[0, 0, 1] - center_src[0, 0, 1]\n\n    # Calculate scale change (zoom)\n    scale_x = np.linalg.norm(transformed_corners[1] - transformed_corners[0]) / w\n    scale_y = np.linalg.norm(transformed_corners[3] - transformed_corners[0]) / h\n    scale = (scale_x + scale_y) / 2\n\n    # Calculate rotation\n    v1 = transformed_corners[1] - transformed_corners[0]\n    angle = np.arctan2(v1[0, 1], v1[0, 0]) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Thresholds\n    translation_threshold = 5  # pixels\n    scale_threshold = 0.02     # 2% change\n    rotation_threshold = 1.5   # degrees\n\n    # Classify motion (prioritize by significance)\n\n    # Check for zoom/dolly\n    if scale > 1 + scale_threshold:\n        return \"Dolly In\"\n    elif scale < 1 - scale_threshold:\n        return \"Dolly Out\"\n\n    # Check for pan (horizontal translation)\n    if abs(dx) > translation_threshold and abs(dx) > abs(dy):\n        if dx > 0:\n            return \"Pan Right\"\n        else:\n            return \"Pan Left\"\n\n    # Check for tilt (vertical translation)\n    if abs(dy) > translation_threshold:\n        if dy > 0:\n            return \"Tilt Down\"\n        else:\n            return \"Tilt Up\"\n\n    # Check for roll (rotation around viewing axis)\n    if abs(angle) > rotation_threshold:\n        if angle > 0:\n            return \"Roll Right\"\n        else:\n            return \"Roll Left\"\n\n    return \"Stay\"\n\n\ndef detect_dynamic_objects(frame1, frame2, H, mask, threshold=10):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect dynamic objects by finding regions that don't follow global camera motion.\n    Returns binary mask where True indicates dynamic objects.\n    \"\"\"\n    h, w = frame1.shape[:2]\n\n    if H is None:\n        # No camera motion detected, use frame differencing\n        gray1 = cv2.cvtColor(frame1, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        diff = cv2.absdiff(gray1, gray2)\n        _, dynamic_mask = cv2.threshold(diff, 25, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n    else:\n        # Warp frame1 to frame2's perspective using homography\n        warped = cv2.warpPerspective(frame1, H, (w, h))\n\n        # Compute difference between warped frame1 and frame2\n        gray_warped = cv2.cvtColor(warped, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        diff = cv2.absdiff(gray_warped, gray2)\n\n        # Threshold to get binary mask\n        _, dynamic_mask = cv2.threshold(diff, threshold, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n\n    # Apply morphological operations to reduce noise\n    kernel = np.ones((5, 5), np.uint8)\n    dynamic_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(dynamic_mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel)\n    dynamic_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(dynamic_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)\n\n    # Further denoise with median blur\n    dynamic_mask = cv2.medianBlur(dynamic_mask, 5)\n\n    return dynamic_mask.astype(bool)\n\n\ndef save_results(motion_labels, dynamic_masks, output_dir=\"/root\"):\n    \"\"\"\n    Save results in the required format:\n    1. pred_instructions.json: frame intervals to motion labels\n    2. pred_dyn_masks.npz: CSR sparse binary masks\n    \"\"\"\n    output_dir = Path(output_dir)\n\n    # Save motion instructions\n    instructions = {}\n    for i, label in enumerate(motion_labels):\n        key = f\"{i}->{i+1}\"\n        instructions[key] = [label]\n\n    with open(output_dir / \"pred_instructions.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(instructions, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Saved motion instructions to {output_dir / 'pred_instructions.json'}\")\n\n    # Save dynamic masks in CSR sparse format\n    if len(dynamic_masks) > 0:\n        h, w = dynamic_masks[0].shape\n        save_dict = {\"shape\": [h, w]}\n\n        for i, mask in enumerate(dynamic_masks):\n            # Convert to CSR sparse matrix\n            sparse_mask = csr_matrix(mask)\n            save_dict[f\"f_{i}_data\"] = sparse_mask.data\n            save_dict[f\"f_{i}_indices\"] = sparse_mask.indices\n            save_dict[f\"f_{i}_indptr\"] = sparse_mask.indptr\n\n        np.savez_compressed(output_dir / \"pred_dyn_masks.npz\", **save_dict)\n        print(f\"Saved dynamic masks to {output_dir / 'pred_dyn_masks.npz'}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    video_path = \"/root/input.mp4\"\n    target_fps = 6\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Video Camera Motion and Dynamic Object Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Step 1: Sample video frames\n    print(\"\\n[1/4] Sampling video frames...\")\n    frames, frame_indices = sample_video(video_path, target_fps)\n\n    if len(frames) < 2:\n        print(\"Error: Not enough frames to analyze\")\n        return\n\n    # Step 2: Estimate camera motion for each frame pair\n    print(\"\\n[2/4] Estimating camera motion...\")\n    motion_labels = []\n    homographies = []\n    masks = []\n\n    for i in range(len(frames) - 1):\n        motion_label, H, mask = estimate_camera_motion(frames[i], frames[i+1])\n        motion_labels.append(motion_label)\n        homographies.append(H)\n        masks.append(mask)\n        print(f\"  Frame {i} -> {i+1}: {motion_label}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Detect dynamic objects\n    print(\"\\n[3/4] Detecting dynamic objects...\")\n    dynamic_masks = []\n\n    # First frame has no previous frame, so create empty mask\n    h, w = frames[0].shape[:2]\n    dynamic_masks.append(np.zeros((h, w), dtype=bool))\n\n    for i in range(len(frames) - 1):\n        dyn_mask = detect_dynamic_objects(frames[i], frames[i+1], homographies[i], masks[i])\n        dynamic_masks.append(dyn_mask)\n        num_dynamic_pixels = np.sum(dyn_mask)\n        print(f\"  Frame {i+1}: {num_dynamic_pixels} dynamic pixels ({100*num_dynamic_pixels/(h*w):.2f}%)\")\n\n    # Step 4: Save results\n    print(\"\\n[4/4] Saving results...\")\n    save_results(motion_labels, dynamic_masks)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Analysis complete!\")\n    print(f\"Generated files:\")\n    print(f\"  - /root/pred_instructions.json ({len(motion_labels)} frame intervals)\")\n    print(f\"  - /root/pred_dyn_masks.npz ({len(dynamic_masks)} frames)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":419,"cache_read_input_tokens":19630,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":419,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c3eac92-f053-4a7b-98b0-a0de66848e88","uuid":"7419b55a-d5de-4e54-88be-f2ca99b74b66"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_01UCX36XHjJK9wmF6LXcMr5n","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /root/analyze_video.py"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c3eac92-f053-4a7b-98b0-a0de66848e88","uuid":"f7452e38-918b-491c-a6e3-7a3d87c6c282","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/root/analyze_video.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nVideo analysis script for camera motion (egomotion) estimation and dynamic object detection.\nSamples video at 6 fps, estimates camera motion between frames, and detects moving objects.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport cv2\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef sample_video(video_path, target_fps=6):\n    \"\"\"Sample video frames at target fps.\"\"\"\n    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))\n    original_fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)\n    frame_interval = int(original_fps / target_fps)\n\n    frames = []\n    frame_indices = []\n    frame_count = 0\n    sample_count = 0\n\n    while True:\n        ret, frame = cap.read()\n        if not ret:\n            break\n\n        if frame_count % frame_interval == 0:\n            frames.append(frame)\n            frame_indices.append(frame_count)\n            sample_count += 1\n\n        frame_count += 1\n\n    cap.release()\n    print(f\"Sampled {len(frames)} frames from {frame_count} total frames\")\n    print(f\"Original FPS: {original_fps}, Target FPS: {target_fps}, Frame interval: {frame_interval}\")\n\n    return frames, frame_indices\n\n\ndef estimate_camera_motion(frame1, frame2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate camera motion between two frames using optical flow and homography.\n    Returns motion label based on the dominant transformation.\n    \"\"\"\n    gray1 = cv2.cvtColor(frame1, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n    gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n\n    # Detect features\n    orb = cv2.ORB_create(nfeatures=1000)\n    kp1, des1 = orb.detectAndCompute(gray1, None)\n    kp2, des2 = orb.detectAndCompute(gray2, None)\n\n    if des1 is None or des2 is None or len(kp1) < 10 or len(kp2) < 10:\n        return \"Stay\", None, None\n\n    # Match features\n    bf = cv2.BFMatcher(cv2.NORM_HAMMING, crossCheck=True)\n    matches = bf.match(des1, des2)\n\n    if len(matches) < 10:\n        return \"Stay\", None, None\n\n    # Sort matches by distance\n    matches = sorted(matches, key=lambda x: x.distance)\n    good_matches = matches[:min(100, len(matches))]\n\n    # Get matched points\n    pts1 = np.float32([kp1[m.queryIdx].pt for m in good_matches])\n    pts2 = np.float32([kp2[m.trainIdx].pt for m in good_matches])\n\n    # Estimate homography\n    H, mask = cv2.findHomography(pts1, pts2, cv2.RANSAC, 5.0)\n\n    if H is None:\n        return \"Stay\", None, None\n\n    # Analyze homography to determine motion type\n    motion_label = classify_camera_motion(H, frame1.shape)\n\n    return motion_label, H, mask\n\n\ndef classify_camera_motion(H, frame_shape):\n    \"\"\"\n    Classify camera motion based on homography matrix.\n    Returns one of: Stay, Dolly In, Dolly Out, Pan Left, Pan Right, Tilt Up, Tilt Down, Roll Left, Roll Right\n    \"\"\"\n    if H is None:\n        return \"Stay\"\n\n    h, w = frame_shape[:2]\n\n    # Get corner points\n    corners = np.float32([[0, 0], [w, 0], [w, h], [0, h]]).reshape(-1, 1, 2)\n    transformed_corners = cv2.perspectiveTransform(corners, H)\n\n    # Calculate translation vector from center\n    center_src = np.float32([[w/2, h/2]]).reshape(-1, 1, 2)\n    center_dst = cv2.perspectiveTransform(center_src, H)\n\n    dx = center_dst[0, 0, 0] - center_src[0, 0, 0]\n    dy = center_dst[0, 0, 1] - center_src[0, 0, 1]\n\n    # Calculate scale change (zoom)\n    scale_x = np.linalg.norm(transformed_corners[1] - transformed_corners[0]) / w\n    scale_y = np.linalg.norm(transformed_corners[3] - transformed_corners[0]) / h\n    scale = (scale_x + scale_y) / 2\n\n    # Calculate rotation\n    v1 = transformed_corners[1] - transformed_corners[0]\n    angle = np.arctan2(v1[0, 1], v1[0, 0]) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Thresholds\n    translation_threshold = 5  # pixels\n    scale_threshold = 0.02     # 2% change\n    rotation_threshold = 1.5   # degrees\n\n    # Classify motion (prioritize by significance)\n\n    # Check for zoom/dolly\n    if scale > 1 + scale_threshold:\n        return \"Dolly In\"\n    elif scale < 1 - scale_threshold:\n        return \"Dolly Out\"\n\n    # Check for pan (horizontal translation)\n    if abs(dx) > translation_threshold and abs(dx) > abs(dy):\n        if dx > 0:\n            return \"Pan Right\"\n        else:\n            return \"Pan Left\"\n\n    # Check for tilt (vertical translation)\n    if abs(dy) > translation_threshold:\n        if dy > 0:\n            return \"Tilt Down\"\n        else:\n            return \"Tilt Up\"\n\n    # Check for roll (rotation around viewing axis)\n    if abs(angle) > rotation_threshold:\n        if angle > 0:\n            return \"Roll Right\"\n        else:\n            return \"Roll Left\"\n\n    return \"Stay\"\n\n\ndef detect_dynamic_objects(frame1, frame2, H, mask, threshold=10):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect dynamic objects by finding regions that don't follow global camera motion.\n    Returns binary mask where True indicates dynamic objects.\n    \"\"\"\n    h, w = frame1.shape[:2]\n\n    if H is None:\n        # No camera motion detected, use frame differencing\n        gray1 = cv2.cvtColor(frame1, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        diff = cv2.absdiff(gray1, gray2)\n        _, dynamic_mask = cv2.threshold(diff, 25, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n    else:\n        # Warp frame1 to frame2's perspective using homography\n        warped = cv2.warpPerspective(frame1, H, (w, h))\n\n        # Compute difference between warped frame1 and frame2\n        gray_warped = cv2.cvtColor(warped, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)\n        diff = cv2.absdiff(gray_warped, gray2)\n\n        # Threshold to get binary mask\n        _, dynamic_mask = cv2.threshold(diff, threshold, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)\n\n    # Apply morphological operations to reduce noise\n    kernel = np.ones((5, 5), np.uint8)\n    dynamic_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(dynamic_mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, kernel)\n    dynamic_mask = cv2.morphologyEx(dynamic_mask, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)\n\n    # Further denoise with median blur\n    dynamic_mask = cv2.medianBlur(dynamic_mask, 5)\n\n    return dynamic_mask.astype(bool)\n\n\ndef save_results(motion_labels, dynamic_masks, output_dir=\"/root\"):\n    \"\"\"\n    Save results in the required format:\n    1. pred_instructions.json: frame intervals to motion labels\n    2. pred_dyn_masks.npz: CSR sparse binary masks\n    \"\"\"\n    output_dir = Path(output_dir)\n\n    # Save motion instructions\n    instructions = {}\n    for i, label in enumerate(motion_labels):\n        key = f\"{i}->{i+1}\"\n        instructions[key] = [label]\n\n    with open(output_dir / \"pred_instructions.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(instructions, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Saved motion instructions to {output_dir / 'pred_instructions.json'}\")\n\n    # Save dynamic masks in CSR sparse format\n    if len(dynamic_masks) > 0:\n        h, w = dynamic_masks[0].shape\n        save_dict = {\"shape\": [h, w]}\n\n        for i, mask in enumerate(dynamic_masks):\n            # Convert to CSR sparse matrix\n            sparse_mask = csr_matrix(mask)\n            save_dict[f\"f_{i}_data\"] = sparse_mask.data\n            save_dict[f\"f_{i}_indices\"] = sparse_mask.indices\n            save_dict[f\"f_{i}_indptr\"] = sparse_mask.indptr\n\n        np.savez_compressed(output_dir / \"pred_dyn_masks.npz\", **save_dict)\n        print(f\"Saved dynamic masks to {output_dir / 'pred_dyn_masks.npz'}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    video_path = \"/root/input.mp4\"\n    target_fps = 6\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Video Camera Motion and Dynamic Object Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Step 3: Detect dynamic objects\n    print(\"\\n[3/4] Detecting dynamic objects...\")\n    dynamic_masks = []\n\n    # First frame has no previous frame, so create empty mask\n    h, w = frames[0].shape[:2]\n    dynamic_masks.append(np.zeros((h, w), dtype=bool))\n\n    for i in range(len(frames) - 1):\n        dyn_mask = detect_dynamic_objects(frames[i], frames[i+1], homographies[i], masks[i])\n        dynamic_masks.append(dyn_mask)\n        num_dynamic_pixels = np.sum(dyn_mask)\n        print(f\"  Frame {i+1}: {num_dynamic_pixels} dynamic pixels ({100*num_dynamic_pixels/(h*w):.2f}%)\")\n\n    # Step 4: Save results\n    print(\"\\n[4/4] Saving results...\")\n    save_results(motion_labels, dynamic_masks)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Analysis complete!\")\n    print(f\"Generated files:\")\n    print(f\"  - /root/pred_instructions.json ({len(motion_labels)} frame intervals)\")\n    print(f\"  - /root/pred_dyn_masks.npz ({len(dynamic_masks)} frames)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
